# Systematic Review Consensus Data Extraction Master Audit Log

- **Review Title**: Protocol characteristics associated with clinically meaningful 24-hour opioid sparing from perioperative transcutaneous electrical acupoint stimulation and electroacupuncture: a systematic review and meta-regression of randomized controlled trials
- **Covidence Review ID**: 799962
- **Lead Investigator**: John Ryan N. Mendoza, RN, MSc (Lund University)
- **Role**: Final Consensus Auditor
- **Primary Principle**: The published source material is the ground truth. No agreement between reviewers is assumed correct without independent verification against the original full-text source.
